                                                                                           this Sunday music selections. Let me know what you want to d
                                                                                           o so we can get that info to Tiffany and in planning center.
                                                                                                                                                       
                                                                                           Hymns & Contemporary Song Suggestions for March 2 (Transfigu
                                                                                           ration Sunday)                                              
                                                                                                                                                       
                                                                                           Theme & Scripture:                                          
                                                                                                   •       Theme: The Transfiguration of Christ, reveal
                                                                                           ing His divine glory and preparing the disciples for His jou
                                                                                           rney to the cross.                                          
                                                                                                   •       Scripture: Matthew 17:1-9 (Jesus is transfig
                                                                                           ured before Peter, James, and John on the mountain, and the 
                                                                                           voice of God declares, “This is my Son, whom I love; listen 
                                                                                           to Him.”)                                                   
                                                                                                                                                       
                                                                                           Bells Selections (Prelude or Congregational Accompaniment)  
                                                                                                   •       “All Glory, Laud, and Honor” (#280 UMH)     
                                                                                                   •       “Jesus Shall Reign” (#157 UMH)              
                                                                                                                                                       
                                                                                           These both fit well with the royalty and divinity of Christ,
                                                                                            making them great for Transfiguration Sunday.              
                                                                                                                                                       
                                                                                           Hymn Suggestions (Congregational Singing)                   
                                                                                                   1.      “Christ, Whose Glory Fills the Skies” (#173 
                                                                                           UMH)                                                        
                                                                                                   •       A classic Transfiguration hymn, celebrating 
                                                                                           the light of Christ shining upon us.                        
                                                                                                   2.      “Fairest Lord Jesus” (#189 UMH)             
                                                                                                   •       Reflects on the beauty and majesty of Christ
                                                                                           , fitting for His revealed glory on the mountain.           
                                                                                                   3.      “Holy, Holy, Holy! Lord God Almighty” (#64 U
                                                                                           MH)                                                         
                                                                                                   •       A powerful Trinitarian hymn, tying into the 
                                                                                           voice of God affirming Jesus as His Son.                    
                                                                                                   4.      “Be Thou My Vision” (#451 UMH)              
                                                                                                   •       Connects well with seeing Christ in His full
                                                                                           ness and listening to Him, as the disciples were called to d
                                                                                           o.                                                          
                                                                                                   5.      “Shine, Jesus, Shine” (#2173 TFWS)          
                                                                                                   •       A modern hymn with a strong Transfiguration 
                                                                                           theme, focusing on the light of Christ shining in us.       
                                                                                                                                                       
                                                                                           Contemporary Worship Songs                                  
                                                                                                   1.      “King of Kings” (Hillsong Worship)          
                                                                                                   •       Tells the story of Christ’s divine mission a
                                                                                           nd reign, fitting the Transfiguration theme.                
                                                                                                   2.      “Open the Eyes of My Heart” (Paul Baloche)  
                                                                                                   •       A great response song—praying to see Christ 
                                                                                           more clearly, just as the disciples did on the mountain.    
                                                                                                   3.      “Jesus, We Love You” (Bethel Music)         
                                                                                                   •       A beautiful worshipful response to seeing th
                                                                                           e glory of Christ.                                          
                                                                                                   4.      “Revelation Song” (Kari Jobe)               
                                                                                                   •       A powerful song about Christ’s majesty, echo
                                                                                           ing the vision of His divine glory.
